Yeah… I mean, sometimes it feels like that, kind of. Like I’ll realize I didn’t eat all day, and then it’s midnight and I’m just going through takeout or chips or whatever’s there, and I’m not really thinking about it.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Yeah… I mean, the closest I can think of is nights after work where I’ll realize I haven’t really eaten all day, and then it’s like after midnight I’m ordering food and just… tearing through it, plus whatever snacks are around.

I don’t know if I’d call it this clear “episode” where I’m sitting there thinking, “I’m losing control.” It’s more like I’m not paying attention, and then suddenly there’s all this food gone and I feel kind of freaked out by it. That’s honestly the part that scared me into coming in—my eating’s just been all over the place. Some days I forget meals completely, and other nights it swings the other way.

Yeah, I mean… I guess that’s the scary part. I don’t know if I’d say I’m totally “not in control,” like that sounds dramatic, but honestly my eating has been all over the place.

Yeah, sort of… I mean, I don’t know if I’d call it totally out of control, but honestly the thing that scared me into coming in is my eating has just been all over the place. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Yeah… I think so, kind of. Like, the thing that honestly scared me enough to come in was my eating being all over the place. Some days I just forget meals completely—like I get caught up in the day, and then suddenly it’s late—and then other nights, after midnight, I’m just tearing through takeout and snacks.

And it’s weird because it’s not like I planned to do that. I’ll just sort of realize I’m already doing it, like I skipped the whole normal part of deciding to eat.

Yeah, I guess… not like I’m blacked out or anything, but it does feel kind of automatic. Honestly that’s what freaked me out enough to come in — my eating’s been all over the place.
